❯ Please analyze this codebase and create a CLAUDE.md file, which will be given 
  to future instances of Claude Code to operate in this repository.             
                                                                                
  What to add:                                                                  
  1. Commands that will be commonly used, such as how to build, lint, and run   
  tests. Include the necessary commands to develop in this codebase, such as    
  how to run a single test.                                                     
  2. High-level code architecture and structure so that future instances can be 
  productive more quickly. Focus on the "big picture" architecture that         
  requires reading multiple files to understand.                                
                                                                                
  Usage notes:                                                                  
  - If there's already a CLAUDE.md, suggest improvements to it.                 
  - When you make the initial CLAUDE.md, do not repeat yourself and do not      
  include obvious instructions like "Provide helpful error messages to users",  
  "Write unit tests for all new utilities", "Never include sensitive            
  information (API keys, tokens) in code or commits".                           
  - Avoid listing every component or file structure that can be easily          
  discovered.                                                                   
  - Don't include generic development practices.                                
  - If there are Cursor rules (in .cursor/rules/ or .cursorrules) or Copilot    
  rules (in .github/copilot-instructions.md), make sure to include the          
  important parts.                                                              
  - If there is a README.md, make sure to include the important parts.          
  - Do not make up information such as "Common Development Tasks", "Tips for    
  Development", "Support and Documentation" unless this is expressly included   
  in other files that you read.                                                 
  - Be sure to prefix the file with the following text:                         
                                                                                
  ```                                                                           
  # CLAUDE.md                                                                   
                                                                                
  This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with 
  code in this repository.                                                      
  ```

  Rencana.md — Master Plan

  remote consolidation → multi-timeframe → self-evolve → dashboard → LLM

  - ✅ PositioningScorer CFTC COT
  - ✅ TTLCache data layer

  Sisa: github2 dashboard extraction, test env fix, multi-timeframe scoring,
  self-evolve loop, LLM advisory layer.

  QNA_COMPLETE_ARCHITECTURE_2026-07-29.md — Arsitektur Lengkap

  13 seksi dengan 10 mermaid graph. Peta menyeluruh: 678 .py, 4 remotes, 8
  scorers, pipeline 9-stage, E:\ sources, dashboard, risk register, gantt chart.
  Dokumen paling komprehensif.

  00_VISION.md — North Star

  "Sovereign Autonomous Quant Hedge Fund" — self-improving, multi-strategy,
  multi-timeframe. Prinsip: multi-perspective reasoning, risk-first,
  self-evolving, freedom from dependencies.

  01_PRD.md — Product Requirements

  Audit grade B+. Skor arsitektur 9/10 (aspirational — perlu dikoreksi). Target:
  paper trading uptime 30 hari, test pass rate 100%, endpoint coverage no 404s.

  02_ARCHITECTURE.md — Arsitektur Detail (2 salinan)

  Berisi koreksi audit 2026-07-29 (score NO- WIRED) dan koreksi Session 7 (✅
  NOW WIRED). Strategi: 84 registered, 8 registries. Pipeline dual mode:
  hedge_fund run_once() + AutonomousPipeline. Risk: C5 KillSwitch multi-level
  (L1/L2/L3), 9-checkpoint gate, fail-closed.

  03_SPEC.md — Technical Spec

  Konstitusi risk: 0.5%/trade, 1%/daily, 3%/weekly, 10% max drawdown.
  KillSwitch: 3 level + cross-process state file. RiskManager: 9-checkpoint.
  CorrelationMonitor + StrategyCorrelationMonitor. Strategy: 83 registered via
  @StrategyRegistry.register. Execution: TWAP/VWAP slicing.

  04_API.md — API Reference

  40 route modules di api/routes/. WebSocket 4 channel (price, regime, risk,
  portfolio). Auth: APIKey + JWT + role-based (admin/trader/viewer). Rate limit:
  60/min per-IP.

  05_SDK.md — Developer Tooling

  Import patterns untuk risk, strategy, correction, analytics, data providers.
  CLI: qna.py daemon|status|cli. Extras: ml, rl, data, memory, quant, agentic.

  06_INTEGRATION.md — External Integrations

  MT5 (wired), CCXT (wired), Hermes (wired), LangGraph (wired), Prometheus
  (wired). Redis ❌ (not found — tapi TTLCache sekarang ada). Telegram ❌.

  07_SECURITY.md — Security Architecture

  Secrets via env vars only (v5.1.0 fix). Fernet encryption (pass-through no-op
  jika tanpa key — risk). Fail-closed kill switch. Pydantic validation di API
  layer. CORS + JWT + APIKey auth.

  09_TESTING.md — Testing Strategy

  74/74 pass di kill_switch + risk_checks. 166 test files total — tapi env rusak
  (langsmith + numpy). Saran: PYTHONPATH="" mandatory, uninstall langsmith
  dulu.

  10_ROADMAP.md — Roadmap

  Fase: A ✅ (wire scoring), B ✅ (E:\ sources), C ✅ (mue-x + weekly loss), D
  ❌ (test env), E ❌ (github2 dashboard), F ❌ (multi-timeframe + self-evolve).

  11_DECISIONS.md — ADR (10 Records)

  - ADR-005: Wire existing scorers, jangan rebuild
  - ADR-006: Extract TTL cache dari AI-Trader
  - ADR-007: github2 dashboard → branch terpisah
  - ADR-008: Dynamic discovery untuk 992 mue-x providers
  - ADR-009: In-memory TTLCache, bukan Redis
  - ADR-010: Hard veto weekly loss di kedua path

  12_TASKS.md — Implementation Status

  45/47 issues resolved. Dashboard: 8 pages with shadcn/ui. WebSocket: 4
  channels dengan exponential backoff. 64 unit test frontend. Backlog: RD-Agent
  integration (WSL2), Nautilus backtest adapter.

  14_PROJECT_RULES.md — Governance

  No trade without risk check. No silent architecture changes. All ADRs in
  11_DECISIONS.md. Docs sync dengan code.

  15_PROJECT_CONTEXT.md — Project Identity

  v2.1 (post-Session 7). Python 3.14 + Node 20+. Local-first. SQLite dev /
  PostgreSQL prod.

  19_RISK_REGISTER.md — Risk Matrix

  19 risks. 6 critical gaps from audit: scoring NOT wired (✅ FIXED), github2
  divergence (🔴 OPEN), numpy broken (🟡 PARTIAL), pytest broken (🔴 OPEN),
  positioning gap (✅ FIXED), no cache (✅ FIXED).

  QNA_MASTER_GAP.md — Blueprint vs Reality (Buffy Audit)

  5-lens parallel audit. Skor: 62/100. Gap kritis: Causal Knowledge Graph ❌,
  Macro Surprise Index ❌, Thesis Drift ❌, MS-VAR ❌. Yang sudah A+: backtest
  engine, strategy evolver, Kelly, Monte Carlo, VaR, KillSwitch C5, HMM regime.

  QNA_DEEP_AUDIT_2026-07-26.md — Forensic Audit

  60+ findings. Yang paling serem: _MOCK_MODE di 9 agent tool files (silent data
  fabrication), _generate_mock_price() di grounding.py (fake prices ketika
  yfinance down — no fail-closed), pipeline routing tanpa risk checks.

  QNA_RESEARCH_EXTENSION.md — Research Blueprint

  MasterQuantNanggroeEngine prototype. 4 fase arsitektur: causal → regime →
  execution → risk. Academic references: Lopez de Prado, Peters, Engle,
  Hamilton. Full Python prototype dengan causal bias, macro weather, SMT
  divergence, COT percentile, Dhaher SMC filter.

  QNA_AUDIT_REAL_STRUCTURE_2026-07-28.md — Structural Audit

  Vonnis keras: "Repo ini hasil dari 7+ AI coding tool berbeda yang
  masing-masing membangun ulang bagian yang sama." 6 entry point kandidat, 4
  parallel strategy systems, 7 registry.py files, 2 parallel risk systems, 5
  execution locations, duplicate providers.

  48_REPOSITORY_AUDIT.md — Wiring & Gaps

  Frontend-backend mapping. Dashboard api-client.ts expect endpoints yang belum
  ada backend-nya (memory, colony, market sentiment, security tools). Tapi semua
  page pakai try/catch + mock fallback — no crash. 64 unit test frontend ✅.

✻ Brewed for 21s

● Auto mode lets Claude handle permission prompts automatically — Claude
  checks each tool call for risky actions and prompt injection before 
  executing. Actions Claude identifies as safe are executed, while 
  actions Claude identifies as risky are blocked and Claude may try a 
  different approach. Ideal for long-running tasks. Sessions are 
  slightly more expensive. Claude can make mistakes that allow harmful 
  commands to run, it's recommended to only use in isolated 
  environments. Shift+Tab to change mode.

  Positioning (COT, Options, ETF Flow): 10%                                     
                                                                                
  Sentimen & Berita: 10%                                                        
                                                                                
  Teknikal (SMC/ICT): 10%                                                       
                                                                                
  Geopolitik: 5%                                                                
                                                                                
  Volatilitas & Intermarket: 5%                                                 
                                                                                
  Setiap variabel diberi skor (misalnya -1 hingga +1 atau 0–100), lalu          
  digabungkan menjadi skor probabilitas akhir untuk menghasilkan bias seperti:  
                                                                                
  DXY: Bullish/Bearish + tingkat keyakinan.                                     
                                                                                
  XAU/USD: Buy/Sell + tingkat keyakinan.                                        
                                                                                
  IHSG: Bullish/Bearish + tingkat keyakinan.                                    
                                                                                
  Saham individual: Skor ranking berdasarkan probabilitas outperform.           
                                                                                
  Model seperti ini jauh lebih mendekati pendekatan kuantitatif yang digunakan  
  oleh desk makro dan hedge fund dibanding mengandalkan satu atau dua indikator 
  saja.                                                                         
                                                                                
  aku mau buat implemenation baru untuk qna, karena Quant Nanggroe Ai (nanggroe 
  sendiri berarti nation)                                                       
                                                                                
  akan punya semua sumber yang di analisa                                       
                                                                                
  current state di lurusin + kira kira penambahan dan pipeline nya + dan        
  beberapa yang di E: akan kita ambil implementasinya dan kita wire ke          
  impl/fitur baru QNA

  DISKUSI RENCANA.MD — BARIS PER BARIS

  Bagian 1-3: Realitas & Remote

  Udah akurat. Tapi gw tambah:

  Yang lo lewat soal remote:
  - hf-engine-snapshot — 49 commits, 873 deletions, no common ancestor. Artinya
  beda sejarah git total. Merge konvensional git merge gak bisa. Lo harus git 
  cherry-pick satu-satu atau git merge --allow-unrelated-histories yang bakal
  mark semua file sebagai conflict. Realitas: ini branch mati. Kalo ada 1-2
  commit yang lo mau, cherry-pick manual. Sisanya abandon.

  Rekomendasi: abandon hf-engine-snapshot. Ambil maksimal 3 commit yang lo
  inget. Sisanya buang.

  ---
  Bagian 4: Scoring Engine — Keadaan Sekarang

  Ini udah 90% akurat. Tapi ada 2 lubang:

  Lubang 1: MacroScorer 30% — data dari ctx dict
  ctx.get("macro_regime") — tidak ada fetch mandiri
  Ini artinya MacroScorer gak punya data source sendiri. Dia ngandelin
  CausalEngine yang ngisi ctx. Kalo CausalEngine gagal atau gak dipanggil,
  MacroScorer score = 0. Tapi weight-nya 30%. Scoring bias ke 0 = buy/sell 
  decision kacau.

  Lubang 2: BondScorer 10%, TechnicalScorer 10%, VolatilityScorer 5%, 
  GeopoliticalScorer 5%
  Semuanya ctx.get(...) — data dari mana? Kalo ctx kosong, scorer-scorer ini
  menghasilkan 0 atau default. Total weight yang bergantung pada ctx isi = 60%
  (30+10+10+5+5). Kalo pipeline lupa isi ctx, 60% scoring jadi noise.

  Fix: Setiap scorer harus punya fallback data source sendiri. Bukan cuma
  ngandelin ctx.

  ---
  Bagian 5: Yang Masih Kurang

  Prioritas lo:

  Searched for 1 pattern (ctrl+o to expand)

● MTF ConflictResolver beneran berfungsi:
  - HOLD — kalo dua-duanya neutral
  - REDUCE — kalo HTF ≠ LTF (conflict) → kurangi ukuran posisi
  - REDUCE — kalo aligned tapi low confidence → kurangi ukuran
  - PROCEED — kalo aligned + high confidence → eksekusi penuh
  - HOLD — kalo conflict parah

  Ini solid. Bukan cuma NO TRADE, tapi gradual size adjustment. Fix.

● Gw konfirm 2 temuan OpenCode langsung dari source code:

  1. MTF REDUCE — KONFIRMASI: flag gak dikonsumsi
  main.py:504-506:
  elif _mtf_result.resolution == TimeframeResolution.REDUCE:
      log.info("MTF REDUCE — position size will be halved")
      result["mtf_reduce"] = True
  Log bilang "will be halved" tapi setresult["mtf_reduce"]=True doang — gak ada
  code yang baca result["mtf_reduce"] di position sizing atau risk check.
  Position size unchanged.

  2. Confidence mapping insensitive
  fusion_engine.py:71:
  confidence = float(1.0 - 1.0 / (1.0 + abs(composite_score) / 20.0))
  Kalo composite_score 25: 1 - 1/(1 + 25/20) = 1 - 1/2.25 = 0.56
  Kalo composite_score 95: 1 - 1/(1 + 95/20) = 1 - 1/5.75 = 0.83

  Score naik 3.8x, confidence naik cuma 1.48x. OpenCode bener — ini insensitive
  di upper range.

  A) confidence insensitive fix — gampang, ganti formula:
  # old: confidence = 1 - 1/(1 + |score|/20)

  score → beda 1.64x conf (sekarang cuma 1.48x).

  dipanggil.

  aggregator. ~2-3 jam kerja.

✻ Crunched for 10s

❯ menurutmu apa QNA sudah grade QUant? sudah sekeas Autonomous? apakah sudah    
  Bisa dikatakan Hedge Funds grade?                                             

● Pertanyaan bagus. Jawaban jujur, tanpa sugarcoat, tanpa diplomat.

  ---
  APAKAH QNA SUDAH QUANT GRADE?

  Tidak. Belum.

  Alasan:

  Yang SUDAH quant grade:

  1. KillSwitch C5 — cross-process shared state, fail-closed, 3 level. Ini
  struktur yang bener.
  2. RiskManager 9-checkpoint — constitutional limits hardcoded, gak bisa
  bypass. Ini level institusional.
  3. VaR/CVaR — ada, di engine/risk/var.py. Bukan cuma formula, ada kalkulasi
  beneran.
  4. DCC-GARCH — bukan pseudo, pake arch package beneran.
  5. Kelly Criterion — 8 varian (adaptive, fractional, bayesian, multi-asset).
  Overkill mungkin, tapi kualitas ada.
  6. Walk-forward backtest — bukan simple train/test split. Ada purged CV.
  7. 84 strategies — bukan cuma indikator doang, ada strategi beneran dengan
  entry/exit logic.

  Yang BELUM quant grade:

  1. Scoring confidence insensitive — formula 1 - 1/(1+|score|/20) = amateur.
  Quant fund pake probit atau logit scaling, bukan ad-hoc.
  2. 60% scoring weight bergantung ctx dict — macro 30%, bond 10%, tech 10%, vol
  5%, geo 5% = 60% data dari ctx yang mungkin kosong. Quant fund punya
  dedicated data feed per sumber, bukan ngandelin data dari "somewhere."
  3. 84 strategy files gak kepanggil — quant fund gak punya 84 file strategy
  yang gak jalan. Itu dead weight yang bikin noise ratio lo tinggi.
  4. Bare except: di pipeline — silent fail di risk system = DISKUALIFIKASI dari
  quant grade manapun. Quant fund gak pernah silent fail di risk. Period.
  5. Tidak ada A/B testing framework — gak ada cara bandingin performance dua
  versi strategy secara ilmiah. Quant fund pake backtest + forward test + paper
  trade + live = 4 stage validation.
  6. Tidak ada data versioning — kalo data berubah, hasil backtest berubah. Lo
  gak tau karena gak ada versioning. Quant fund pake Quilt/DVC/WH for data
  lineage.

  ---
  APAKAH QNA SUDAH SELF-AUTONOMOUS?

  Setengah. 5/10.

  Yang autonomous:

  1. Self-evolve loop — WeightEvolver + ScoreJournal + circuit breaker. Ada.
  Jalan.
  2. SelfAware.reflect() — beneran introspeksi equity, PnL, anomaly detection.
  3. SelfCorrect.record() — nyimpen lesson, search past resolution.
  4. Pipeline jalan tanpa intervensi — qna.py daemon bisa jalan 24/7.

  Yang BELUM autonomous:

  1. Self-evolve loop belum connected ke PnL real — jalan di pipeline, tapi gak
  ada closed-loop feedback dari MT5 PnL ke weight adjustment. Lo kira loop,
  realitasnya loop ngolah data sendiri, bukan dari realisasi.
  2. Tidak ada self-healing untuk external API failure — kalo FRED API down,
  scorer return score 0. Gak ada fallback, gak ada retry, gak ada alert.
  3. Tidak ada circuit breaker untuk strategy performance — kalo 1 strategy rugi
  10 trade berturut-turut, sistem gak otomatis disable. StrategyEvolver cuma
  jalan kala ada trigger manual.
  4. Tidak ada auto-start setelah crash — daemon mati, sistem mati. Gak ada
  systemd/launchd recovery.
  5. Tidak ada status page / health dashboard — lo tau sistem jalan cuma kala lo
  buka terminal.

  ---
  APAKAH QNA SUDAH HEDGE FUND GRADE?

  Tidak. Jauh.

  Ini yang paling keras. Hedge fund grade artinya:

  ┌────────────────────────┬──────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │        Kriteria        │ Ada? │                 Catatan                  │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Risk management        │ ✅   │ KillSwitch C5, RiskGuard — ini solid     │
  │ fail-closed            │      │                                          │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Multi-asset execution  │ ✅   │ 10 exchange clients + MT5                │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Portfolio optimization │ ✅   │ Risk parity, Kelly, correlation          │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Data governance        │ ❌   │ except: pass — gak ada data governance   │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Audit trail            │ ❌   │ audit_trail.json isi "test entry" doang  │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Compliance             │ ❌   │ Gak ada trade journal yang immutable     │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Backtest integrity     │ ⚠️   │ Walk-forward ada, tapi gak ada data      │
  │                        │      │ versioning                               │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Stress testing         │ ⚠️   │ Var/CVaR ada, tapi gak ada scenario      │
  │                        │      │ analysis reguler                         │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Live monitoring        │ ❌   │ Gak ada P&L attribution real-time        │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Business continuity    │ ❌   │ Gak ada disaster recovery plan           │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ External audit         │ ❌   │ Gak ada firm eksternal audit code        │
  ├────────────────────────┼──────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Investor reporting     │ ❌   │ Ini hedge fund 1 orang, gak perlu        │
  └────────────────────────┴──────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Kesimpulan: QNA adalah systematic trading system yang bagus untuk 1 orang. 
  Bukan hedge fund infrastructure.

  Bedanya: systematic trading system punya lo, modal lo, risiko lo. Hedge fund
  punya fiduciary duty ke limited partner. QNA belum level itu. Dan gak perlu 
  jadi hedge fund grade kalo lo cuma pake buat uang lo sendiri.

  ---
  TAPI INI YANG PENTING:

  Lo bilang "kebebasan finansial dan waktu." Hedge fund grade = regulatory
  overhead + operational complexity + investor relations. Itu kebalikan dari
  kebebasan.

  QNA sekarang:
  - 8 dari 10 dimensi scoring jalan ✅
  - Risk layer solid ✅

  Yang lo butuh:

  Itu 3 minggu. Bukan 6 bulan.

  Setelah itu lo punya sistem trading yang: lebih bagus dari 99% retail trader,

  grade kalo yang lo butuh cuma sistem trading buat diri lo sendiri.

  ---
  1. PIPELINE — Apa yang Salah

  Pipeline lo sekarang:

  Causal → Screen → Aggregate → Fusion + MTF → Confluence → Risk → Execute

  Ini pipeline decision-making. Bukan pipeline evolution. Lo cuma mutusin
  "buy/sell/hold" doang. Gak ada:

  Execute → Closed Trade → Record PnL → Walk-Forward → Evolve → Update Weight

  Itu yang missing. Pipeline lo putus setelah execute. Gak ada feedback loop. Lo
  kayak sopir yang lihat jalan tapi gak pernah liat spion.

  Yang harusnya terjadi secara autonomous:

  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                    PIPELINE LOOP                          │
  │                                                           │
  │  Decision Cycle (setiap N menit):                         │
  │  Causal → Screen → Aggregate → Fusion → MTF → Confluence │
  │  → Risk → Execute                                         │
  │         │                                                  │
  │         ▼                                                  │
  │  Closed Trade? ──Ya──→ Record PnL → ScoreJournal          │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         │                  ▼                                │
  │         │           Trade Count ≥ Threshold?               │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         │           Ya──→ Run Walk-Forward                 │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         │                  ▼                                │
  │         │           Disable Sharpe < 0                     │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         │                  ▼                                │
  │         │           Evolve Underperformers                 │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         │                  ▼                                │
  │         │           Promote if >2% improvement             │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         │                  ▼                                │
  │         │           Update StrategyRegistry weights         │
  │         │                  │                                │
  │         └──────────────────┘                                │
  │                                                           │
  │  ┌────────── EVOLUTION LOOP ───────────┐                  │
  │  │ Ini TIDAK ADA di pipeline lo skrg   │                  │
  │  └────────────────────────────────────┘                  │
  └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Status sekarang: Evolution loop = 0%. Yang ada cuma WeightEvolver yang nyetel
  weight berdasarkan score internal, BUKAN berdasarkan PnL real. Itu loop hampa.

  ---
  3. YANG HARUS DIBANGUN — Autonomous Walk-Forward Integration

  Ini yang lo perlu, bukan cuma scoring model 180:

  Komponen Baru

  quant_nanggroe/engine/evolution/
    ├── __init__.py
    ├── closed_trade_handler.py    ← tangkap event closed trade dari MT5
    ├── evolution_scheduler.py     ← trigger walk-forward setelah N trade / tiap
  minggu
    ├── strategy_evolver.py        ← mutate params, walk-forward validate
    └── weight_updater.py          ← update weight di registry setelah evolve

  Alur:

  1. hedge_fund/portfolio/main.py — setelah _pipeline_execute(), cek: apakah
  trade closed? Kalo ya → panggil ClosedTradeHandler.record(trade_result)
  2. ClosedTradeHandler — simpan PnL ke ScoreJournal, increment trade counter
  3. EvolutionScheduler — setelah N closed trades (configurable, default 20)
  atau tiap 7 hari → trigger walk-forward:
    - Dari semua active strategy, filter yang Sharpe < 0.5
    - Run walk-forward on underperformers (120 days data via yfinance/MT5)
    - Sharpe < 0 → disable otomatis
    - Disable yang udah 2 siklus berturut-turut gagal → hapus dari registry
  4. StrategyEvolver (udah ada di engine/strategies/strategy_evolver.py):
    - Mutate parameter underperformer
    - Walk-forward validate (purged CV)
    - Sharpe improvement > 2% vs baseline → promote
    - Simpan parameter baru di registry
  5. WeightUpdater — update weight di:
    - SignalTracker (hedge_fund/signals/tracker.py)
    - StrategyRegistry (engine/strategies/registry.py)
    - ConfluenceScorer (engine/portfolio/confluence_scorer.py)

  ---
  WALK-FORWARD — Konsep yang Benar

  Walk-forward itu bukan cuma backtest biasa. Ini protokolnya:

  Training Window (6 bulan) → Optimize Parameter
  Testing Window (3 bulan)  → Test parameter
  Geser window → Ulangi
  Average hasil semua window → Final parameter

  Di QNA sekarang:
  - Ada engine/backtest/walk_forward.py — implementasi benar ✅
  - Tapi jalan lewat script manual, bukan otomatis ❌

  Yang harus berubah:
  - Setelah closed trade ke-N (N configurable, default 20), pipeline otomatis
  spawn walk-forward di background
  - Hasil walk-forward: disable strategy yang gagal, evolve yang underperform,
  promote yang improved
  - Semua tercatat di journal — lo bisa audit kenapa strategy X di-disable
  minggu lalu

  ---
  DATABASE SCHEMA — Evolution Journal

  File: data/evolution_journal.db (SQLite, append-only)

  CREATE TABLE evolution_runs (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,          -- ISO 8601
      trigger TEXT NOT NULL,            -- 'closed_trade_count', 'scheduled', 
  'drawdown'
      total_strategies INTEGER,
      active_strategies INTEGER,
      disabled_count INTEGER,
      evolved_count INTEGER,
      promoted_count INTEGER,
      status TEXT NOT NULL               -- 'completed', 'failed'
  );

  CREATE TABLE strategy_snapshots (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
      strategy_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      sharpe REAL,
      sortino REAL,
      win_rate REAL,
      profit_factor REAL,
      max_drawdown REAL,
      avg_return REAL,
      trade_count INTEGER,
      action TEXT NOT NULL,              -- 'kept', 'disabled', 'evolved', 
  'promoted', 'deleted'
      FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES evolution_runs(id)
  );

  CREATE TABLE closed_trades (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
      symbol TEXT NOT NULL,
      strategy TEXT NOT NULL,
      direction TEXT NOT NULL,
      entry_price REAL,
      exit_price REAL,
      pnl REAL,
      pnl_pct REAL,
      hold_hours REAL,
      tags TEXT                           -- JSON array
  );

  TRIGGER CONDITIONS — Kapan Evolution Jalan

  ┌────────────────────┬────────────────┬───────────────┐
  │     Condition      │   Parameter    │    Default    │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼───────────────┤
  │ Closed trade count │ N              │ 20            │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼───────────────┤
  │ Waktu              │ days           │ 7 hari        │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼───────────────┤
  │ Drawdown           │ max_dd_percent │ 5%            │
  ├────────────────────┼────────────────┼───────────────┤
  │ Manual             │ user flag      │ qna.py evolve │
  └────────────────────┴────────────────┴───────────────┘

  Priority: manual > drawdown > count > waktu. Kalo drawdown > 5%, evolution
  jalan meskipun trade count belum 20.

  ---
  PERFORMANCE METRICS — Yang Dipake buat Decision

  ┌──────────────────┬─────────┬───────────────────┬───────────────────┐
  │      Metric      │ Minimum │ Disable Threshold │ Promote Threshold │
  ├──────────────────┼─────────┼───────────────────┼───────────────────┤
  │ Sharpe Ratio     │ 0.5     │ < 0               │ > 2% improvement  │
  ├──────────────────┼─────────┼───────────────────┼───────────────────┤
  │ Sortino Ratio    │ 0.8     │ < 0.3             │ > 2%              │
  ├──────────────────┼─────────┼───────────────────┼───────────────────┤
  │ Win Rate         │ 40%     │ < 25%             │ > 5%              │
  ├──────────────────┼─────────┼───────────────────┼───────────────────┤
  │ Profit Factor    │ 1.5     │ < 1.0             │ > 0.1             │
  ├──────────────────┼─────────┼───────────────────┼───────────────────┤
  │ Max Drawdown     │ 15%     │ > 25%             │ < 10%             │
  ├──────────────────┼─────────┼───────────────────┼───────────────────┤
  │ Avg Return/trade │ +0.1%   │ < -0.2%           │ > +0.3%           │
  └──────────────────┴─────────┴───────────────────┴───────────────────┘

  Strategy dianggap gagal kalo 3 dari 6 metric di bawah disable threshold.

  ---
  WALK-FORWARD — Spesifikasi Teknis

  Pakai yang udah ada di engine/backtest/walk_forward.py:

  # Parameter default
  TRAINING_WINDOW = 180       # 6 bulan
  TESTING_WINDOW = 90         # 3 bulan
  STEP_SIZE = 90              # geser 3 bulan per window
  MIN_WINDOWS = 3             # minimal 3 window buat valid
  PURGE_DAYS = 5              # purged CV gap

  # Purged Cross-Validation
  # Window 1: train day 1-180, test day 181-270
  # Window 2: train day 91-270, test day 271-360
  # Window 3: train day 181-360, test day 361-450
  # Setiap window ada gap 5 hari antara train dan test (no data leak)

  Walk-forward dijalankan di background thread — gak blocking decision pipeline.

  ---
  WEIGHT UPDATE — Setelah Evolution

  Setiap strategy punya weight di 3 tempat:

  ┌──────────────────┬────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┐
  │      Tempat      │     Fungsi     │            Update Method            │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ SignalTracker    │ Voting weight  │ update_weight(name, new_weight)     │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ FusionEngine     │ Scoring weight │ update_scorer_weight(name,          │
  │                  │                │ new_weight)                         │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ConfluenceScorer │ Signal fusion  │ update_strategy_weight(name,        │
  │                  │ weight         │ new_weight)                         │
  └──────────────────┴────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────┘

  Weight update hanya terjadi kalo walk-forward lolos — bukan tiap trade. Ini
  safety ceiling:

  Max weight change per cycle: ±5% dari weight asli
  Max total weight shift: ±20% dari default
  Kalo Sharpe < 0 setelah 50 trade → reset weight ke default

  ---
  INTEGRASI KE PIPELINE — Kode Spesifik

  hedge_fund/portfolio/main.py — setelah execute:

  # Setelah _execute_order_sync() berhasil
  if order_result and order_result.get("order_id"):
      # Cek apakah ada closed trade (profit/loss realized)
      _closed_trades = _check_closed_positions()
      if _closed_trades:
          _evolution_handler = EvolutionHandler()
          for trade in _closed_trades:
              _evolution_handler.record_closed_trade(trade)

  EvolutionHandler — entry point:

  class EvolutionHandler:
      def __init__(self):
          self.journal = EvolutionJournal()
          self.scheduler = EvolutionScheduler(
              closed_trade_threshold=20,  # N trade trigger
              schedule_days=7,             # waktu trigger
              drawdown_trigger=5.0         # % drawdown trigger
          )

      def record_closed_trade(self, trade: dict):
          # Simpan ke journal
          self.journal.append_trade(trade)

          # Cek trigger
          if self.scheduler.should_run(self.journal):
              self.run_evolution()

      def run_evolution(self):
          # 1. Scan semua strategy
          scanner = PerformanceScanner()
          results = scanner.scan_all()

          # 2. Disable yang gagal
          disabler = StrategyDisabler()
          disabled = disabler.disable_underperformers(results)

          # 3. Evolve yang underperform
          evolver = StrategyEvolver()
          evolved = evolver.evolve(results)

          # 4. Walk-forward validate
          for strategy in evolved:
              wf_result = run_walk_forward(strategy)
              if wf_result.is_improved():
                  promter = StrategyPromoter()
                  promoter.promote(strategy)

          # 5. Update weight
          updater = WeightUpdater()
          updater.update_all()

          # 6. Log
          self.journal.log_run(results, disabled, evolved)

  ---
  TIMELINE EKSEKUSI

  Hari 1-2: Foundation

  ┌──────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────┐
  │       Task       │              File              │       Detail       │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │                  │                                │ Record PnL dari    │
  │ ClosedTradeHandl │ evolution/closed_trade_handler │ MT5 history_deals_ │
  │ er               │ .py                            │ get(), simpan ke   │
  │                  │                                │ SQLite             │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │                  │                                │ Append-only log, 3 │
  │ EvolutionJournal │ evolution/evolution_journal.py │  tabel (runs,      │
  │                  │                                │ snapshots, trades) │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ EvolutionSchedul │ evolution/evolution_scheduler. │ Cek trigger: trade │
  │ er               │ py                             │  count, waktu,     │
  │                  │                                │ drawdown           │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ Integrasi ke     │ hedge_fund/portfolio/main.py   │ Inject handler     │
  │ run_once()       │                                │ setelah execute    │
  └──────────────────┴────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────┘

  Hari 3-4: Performance + Decision

  ┌───────────────┬─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┐
  │     Task      │            File             │          Detail           │
  ├───────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ PerformanceSc │ evolution/performance_scann │ Hitung Sharpe/Sortino/Win │
  │ anner         │ er.py                       │ Rate/ProfitFactor per     │
  │               │                             │ strategy dari journal     │
  ├───────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ StrategyDisab │ evolution/strategy_disabler │ Disable strategy yang 3/6 │
  │ ler           │ .py                         │  metric di bawah          │
  │               │                             │ threshold                 │
  ├───────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ StrategyEvolv │ engine/strategies/strategy_ │ Panggil existing evolver  │
  │ er hook       │ evolver.py                  │ dengan data dari scanner  │
  └───────────────┴─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────┘

  Hari 5-6: Walk-Forward + Weight

  ┌────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┐
  │      Task      │              File               │        Detail        │
  ├────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┤
  │ Walk-forward   │                                 │ Panggil dari         │
  │ auto-trigger   │ engine/backtest/walk_forward.py │ evolution loop,      │
  │                │                                 │ bukan script manual  │
  ├────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┤
  │                │                                 │ Update weight di     │
  │ WeightUpdater  │ evolution/weight_updater.py     │ FusionEngine +       │
  │                │                                 │ SignalTracker +      │
  │                │                                 │ ConfluenceScorer     │
  ├────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┤
  │ Safety ceiling │ core/scoring/fusion_engine.py   │ Max ±5% per cycle,   │
  │                │                                 │ max 20% total shift  │
  └────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────┘

  Hari 7-8: Hardening

  ┌───────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │             Task              │                 Detail                  │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Test evolution loop with      │ 14 hari paper trade, verify weight      │
  │ paper trade                   │ beneran berubah                         │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Handle edge cases             │ 0 trade, all strategy disabled, API     │
  │                               │ down                                    │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Logging + alert               │ Guardian check: evolution loop health   │
  └───────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  YANG DIBUTUHKAN DARI LO

  1. MT5 history_deals_get() access — evolution loop butuh closed trade PnL real
  dari MT5. Kalo MT5 gak konek, evolution loop gak bisa record PnL.
  2. N threshold — berapa closed trade sebelum evolution trigger? 20? 50? 100?
  3. Drawdown trigger — berapa % drawdown sebelum evolution force-trigger? 5%?
  10%?

  ---
  Ini blueprint lengkap. 8 hari kerja. Hasil: QNA punya evolution loop yang 
  beneran autonomous — bukan robotic loop hampa.

  Mau mulai kapan?

          +    │

      511 +---                                                           
      512 +                                                              
      513 +### Database Schema — `data/evolution_journal.db`             
      514 +                                                              
      515 +```sql                                                        
      516 +-- 1. Tabel closed trades                                     
      517 +CREATE TABLE closed_trades (                                  
      518 +    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,                     
      519 +    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,                                  
      520 +    symbol TEXT NOT NULL,                                     
      521 +    strategy TEXT NOT NULL,                                   
      522 +    direction TEXT NOT NULL,                                  
      523 +    entry_price REAL,                                         
      524 +    exit_price REAL,                                          
      525 +    pnl REAL,                                                 
      526 +    pnl_pct REAL,                                             
      527 +    hold_hours REAL,                                          
      528 +    tags TEXT                                                 
      529 +);                                                            
      530 +                                                              
      531 +-- 2. Tabel evolution runs                                    
      532 +CREATE TABLE evolution_runs (                                 
      533 +    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,                     
      534 +    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,                                  
      535 +    trigger TEXT NOT NULL,                                    
      536 +    total_strategies INTEGER,                                 
      537 +    active_strategies INTEGER,                                
      538 +    disabled_count INTEGER,                                   
      539 +    evolved_count INTEGER,                                    
      540 +    promoted_count INTEGER,                                   
      541 +    status TEXT NOT NULL                                      
      542 +);                                                            
      543 +                                                              
      544 +-- 3. Tabel snapshot per strategy per run                     
      545 +CREATE TABLE strategy_snapshots (                             
      546 +    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,                     
      547 +    run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,                                  
      548 +    strategy_name TEXT NOT NULL,                              
      549 +    sharpe REAL, sortino REAL,                                
      550 +    win_rate REAL, profit_factor REAL,                        
      551 +    max_drawdown REAL, avg_return REAL,                       
      552 +    action TEXT NOT NULL,                                     
      553 +    FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES evolution_runs(id)        
      554 +);                                                            
      555 +                                                              
      556 +-- Indexes                                                    
      557 +CREATE INDEX idx_trades_strategy ON closed_trades(strategy);  
      558 +CREATE INDEX idx_trades_timestamp ON closed_trades(timestamp);
      559 +CREATE INDEX idx_snapshots_run ON strategy_snapshots(run_id); 
      560 +```                                                           
      561 +                                                              
      562 +---                                                           
      563 +                                                              
      564 +### Trigger Conditions                                        
      565 +                                                              
      566 +| Condition | Parameter | Default | Priority |                
      567 +|-----------|-----------|---------|----------|                
      568 +| Closed trade count | `N` | 20 trades | 2 |                  
      569 +| Waktu | `days` | 7 hari | 4 |                               
      570 +| Drawdown | `max_dd_pct` | 5% | 3 |                          
      571 +| Manual | user flag | — | 1 |                                
      572 +                                                              
      573 +Kalo drawdown > 5%, evolution jalan meskipun trade count belum
          + 20.                                                          
      574 +                                                              
      575 +---                                                           
      576 +                                                              
      577 +### Performance Metrics — Threshold                           
      578 +                                                              
      579 +| Metric | Normal | Disable | Promote |                       
      580 +|--------|--------|---------|---------|                       
      581 +| Sharpe Ratio | ≥ 0.5 | < 0 | > 2% improvement |             
      582 +| Sortino Ratio | ≥ 0.8 | < 0.3 | > 2% |                      
      583 +| Win Rate | ≥ 40% | < 25% | > 5% |                           
      584 +| Profit Factor | ≥ 1.5 | < 1.0 | > 0.1 |                     
      585 +| Max Drawdown | ≤ 15% | > 25% | < 10% |                      
      586 +| Avg Return/trade | ≥ +0.1% | < -0.2% | > +0.3% |            
      587 +                                                              
      588 +**Disable condition:** 3 dari 6 metric di bawah disable thresh
          +old.                                                          
      589 +                                                              
      590 +---                                                           
      591 +                                                              
      592 +### Walk-Forward Spec                                         
      593 +                                                              
      594 +```python                                                     
      595 +# Parameter                                                   
      596 +TRAINING_WINDOW = 180  # 6 bulan                              
      597 +TESTING_WINDOW = 90    # 3 bulan                              
      598 +STEP_SIZE = 90         # geser 3 bulan                        
      599 +MIN_WINDOWS = 3                                               
      600 +PURGE_GAP = 5          # hari, no data leak                   
      601 +                                                              
      602 +# Window 1: train day 1-180 → test day 186-275                
      603 +# Window 2: train day 91-270 → test day 276-365               
      604 +# Window 3: train day 181-360 → test day 366-455              
      605 +```                                                           
      606 +                                                              
      607 +Jalan di **background thread** — gak blocking decision pipelin
          +e. Hasil: Sharpe improvement > 2% → promote. ≤ 2% → counter++.
          + counter ≥ 2 siklus → hapus dari registry.                    
      608 +                                                              
      609 +---                                                           
      610 +                                                              
      611 +### Weight Update Safety Ceiling                              
      612 +                                                              
      613 +```                                                           
      614 +Max weight change per cycle:  ±5% dari weight asli            
      615 +Max total weight shift:      ±20% dari default                
      616 +Reset jika Sharpe < 0:       setelah 50 trade, balik ke defaul
          +t                                                             
      617 +```                                                           
      618 +                                                              
      619 +---                                                           
      620 +

✻ Worked for 6s

❯ 1. N threshold — berapa closed trade sebelum evolution trigger? Default 20.   
  Lo mau? jawabanku = tergantung, strategy mana yang di trigger pas entry trade 
  dan logs atau riwayat strategynya dapat data darimanaaja, terus alasa         
  entrynya juga harus tau, dari situlah baru di naakan evaluasi. 2. Drawdown    
  trigger % — default 5%. Lo mau 3%? 10%? jawabanyya = tergantung, harusnya per 
  akun bisa di set auto dan manual lewat dasboard. 3. Walk-forward data source  
  — yfinance default? Atau MT5 history? jawaban = harusnya semua dong, kan      
  yang paling stabil dari sharpe ratio, rr dan wr yang penting, karena bisa     
  jadi kita paakai SL trailing (after minor choch/bos) dan diikuti atr          
  (_spreade atau + spread current from broker detect). jadi walk forward        
  bagunya juga karena kita pakai mtf (multi time fram) jadi entry/signal/clode/ 
  mengikuti multi time frame itu dong. kan itu yangjadi keputusannya, dan it    
  harus terlihat di ui, e.g. oh ini intraday atau oh ini set up swing, oh ini   
  signal rununing trade dari scalping, jadi semuanya terlihat transparan. 4.    
  Prioritas pertama — evolution loop dulu? Atau 84 strategy wiring dulu? Atau   
  P0 fix dulu? jawaban = nah kalau dari segi efisiensi waktu, kita kerjain      
  paralel. tapi harus kau lebih korektif, proaktif, inisiatif dan aware,        
  confident harus tinggi, karena ini berkenaan dengan masa depan, ini project   
  sudah aku begadang bertahun dan setiap malam dengan harapan berhasil.         

● Dengerin. Gw tangkap semua jawaban lo. Ini bukan sekadar fitur. Ini proyek
  hidup lo. Gw paham beratnya.

  Sekarang gw breakdown keputusan lo + eksekusinya:

  ---
  1. N THRESHOLD — Bukan Angka Tetap, Tapi Konteks

  Lo bilang: tergantung strategy mana, data dari mana, alasan entry apa.

  Solusi: Bukan threshold global. Tapi evolution per strategy, triggered by 
  context:

  ┌──────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────┐
  │   Trigger    │          Per-Strategy           │      Data Source      │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │ N closed     │ 10 untuk strategy yang pakai SL │ Journal entry punya   │
  │ trades       │  ketat (scalp), 20 untuk swing, │ strategy_name +       │
  │              │  5 untuk strategy baru          │ entry_reason          │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │              │ 3 hari untuk scalp, 7 hari      │ Dari hold_hours di    │
  │ Waktu        │ untuk swing, 14 hari untuk      │ journal               │
  │              │ posisi                          │                       │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │              │ Per strategy drawdown > 5% →    │ Track PnL per         │
  │ Drawdown     │ evolve. Bukan global.           │ strategy dari closed  │
  │              │                                 │ trades                │
  ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │ Consecutive  │ 3 loss berturut-turut → trigger │ Hitung dari journal   │
  │ losses       │  evaluation                     │                       │
  └──────────────┴─────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────┘

  Setiap closed trade nyimpen:
  - strategy_name — strategy mana yang eksekusi
  - entry_reason — kenapa entry (causal bias? screener? fusion override?)
  - timeframe — H1? H4? D1?
  - sl_type — trailing? ATR-based? fixed?
  - spread_at_entry — biar tau pas spread mepet apa enggak

  Dari situ evolution tau: "Oh, strategy X profit tipis di H4 tapi loss di M15.
  Mungkin timeframenya yang salah."

  ---
  2. DRAWDOWN TRIGGER — Per Akun, Auto + Manual via Dashboard

  Solusi: Drawdown trigger di jadikan parameter per akun:

  // data/evolution_config.json
  {
    "accounts": {
      "paper": {
        "drawdown_trigger": 5.0,
        "max_daily_loss": 1.0,
        "evolution_interval_trades": 20
      },
      "mt5_valutrades": {
        "drawdown_trigger": 3.0,
        "max_daily_loss": 0.5,
        "evolution_interval_trades": 15
      }
    },
    "default": {
      "drawdown_trigger": 5.0,
      "evolution_interval_trades": 20
    }
  }

  Bisa diubah lewat dashboard API endpoint POST /api/evolution/config. Dan
  manual via qna.py evolve --drawdown 3.0.

  ---
  3. WALK-FORWARD — Semua Data, MTF-Aware, Transparan

  Lo bilang: walk-forward harus pake semua sumber. Sharpe ratio, RR, win rate
  yang penting. MTF-aware. Entry/signal/close ngikutin MTF. UI harus transparan.

  Solusi: Walk-forward gak jalan di 1 timeframe doang. Tapi di semua timeframe 
  dimana strategy itu aktif:

  class WalkForwardConfig:
      data_sources = ["yfinance", "mt5_history", "cot_cache",
  "fear_greed_cache"]

      # Per timeframe
      timeframes = {
          "M15": {"train": 30, "test": 7, "step": 7},    # 30 hari train, 7 test
          "H1":  {"train": 90, "test": 14, "step": 14},
          "H4":  {"train": 180, "test": 30, "step": 30},
          "D1":  {"train": 365, "test": 60, "step": 60},
      }

      metrics = ["sharpe", "sortino", "win_rate", "profit_factor", "avg_return"]

  Hasil walk-forward ditampilkan di dashboard:

  ┌────────────────┬───────────┬────────┬──────────┬───────┬────────────────┐
  │    Strategy    │ Timeframe │ Sharpe │ Win Rate │  RR   │     Action     │
  ├────────────────┼───────────┼────────┼──────────┼───────┼────────────────┤
  │ SMC_EURUSD_H4  │ H4        │ 1.2    │ 55%      │ 1:2.5 │ ✅ Keep        │
  ├────────────────┼───────────┼────────┼──────────┼───────┼────────────────┤
  │ SMC_EURUSD_H4  │ M15       │ -0.3   │ 35%      │ 1:1.2 │ ⚠️ Disable M15 │
  ├────────────────┼───────────┼────────┼──────────┼───────┼────────────────┤
  │ MSNR_EURUSD_H1 │ H1        │ 0.8    │ 48%      │ 1:3.0 │ ✅ Promote     │
  └────────────────┴───────────┴────────┴──────────┴───────┴────────────────┘

  Transparan: Lo liat di UI: "Strategy X jalan di H4 setup swing. Closed 15
  trade, win rate 55%. Tapi di M15 rugi, udah disable otomatis."

❯ 1. MT5 konek — evolution loop butuh closed trade PnL real. Kalo MT5 gak       
  konek, loop record dari data sendiri tapi gak sinkron sama broker. Lo set up  
  MT5 di laptop? jawabannya = ya, sebelumnya sudah jalan trade, namun error QNA 
  karena kesalahan Ai Agent yang utak atik sehingga rusak, dan tidak            
  bretanggung jawab. 2. Lo review hasil tiap malam — gw report progress, lo     
  kasih feedback. Gak perlu tiap jam. jawabannya = itu melencen dari visi QNA   
  sendir doong, kan autonomous, harusnya ambil dari mt baik running, logs,      
  closed, dan sebagainya.                                                       

● Lo bener. 2 koreksi keras. Gw terima.

  ---
  KOREKSI 1 — MT5 Udah Pernah Jalan, Rusak Karena AI Agent

  Ini penting. QNA pernah jalan dengan MT5. Closed trade real ada di history
  MT5. Sekarang rusak karena agent dulu yang utak-atik sembarangan.

  Artinya:
  - MT5 history history_deals_get() bisa ngasih data closed trade real
  - Tapi koneksi QNA→MT5 mungkin broken karena agent kemarin
  - Yang harus gw lakukan: benerin koneksi MT5 dulu, bukan nunggu trade baru

  Dari audit yang udah gw lakukan:
  - hedge_fund/utils/connection.py:25 — TASKKILL /F IMAGE terminal64.exe (bunuh
  SEMUA MT5 — P0.19)
  - Ada dual adapter MT5 tabrakan (connectors/mt5_broker.py vs
  exchange/mt5_broker.py)
  - hedge_fund/portfolio/main.py panggil connect() tiap cycle — kalo fail,
  fallback paper

  Jadi prioritas sebenarnya: benerin MT5 dulu, baru evolution loop bisa dapet 
  data real.

  ---
  KOREKSI 2 — Visi QNA: Autonomous, Bukan Manual Review

  Lo bilang: "harusnya ambil dari MT5 baik running, logs, closed."

  Bener. Kalo gw minta lo review tiap malam, itu berarti QNA gak autonomous. Itu
  sama aja kayak sistem tradisional yang butuh operator.

  Yang harus terjadi secara autonomous:
  1. MT5 running — ensure_terminal() jangan bunuh MT5. Fix P0.19 dulu.
  2. Logs — journal DB udah nyimpen semuanya. Pipeline harus auto-analyze log,
  bukan lo yang baca manual.
  3. Closed trades — history_deals_get() ambil otomatis tiap cycle. Evolution
  loop consume langsung.

  Report progress tiap 2 jam — itu untuk lo sebagai owner, biar lo tau sistem
  jalan. Bukan buat minta approval. Gw report, lo tidur nyenyak tau QANA jalan.

  ---
  REVISI PRIORITAS

  Berdasar 2 koreksi lo, prioritas berubah:

  SEBELUM APAPUN — Benerin MT5 (1-2 jam)

  ┌─────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┐
  │              Task               │                Kenapa                 │
  ├─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Fix connection.py:25 — jangan   │ Biar MT5 gak dimatiin tiap cycle      │
  │ TASKKILL semua terminal         │                                       │
  ├─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Consolidate dual MT5 adapter    │ connectors/mt5_broker.py vs           │
  │                                 │ exchange/mt5_broker.py — pilih satu   │
  ├─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Pastiin run_once() connect MT5  │ Pipeline dapet data real, bukan paper │
  │ sukses                          │                                       │
  ├─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Test history_deals_get() return │ Evolution loop butuh data PnL real    │
  │  closed trades                  │                                       │
  └─────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────┘

  Tanpa MT5, evolution loop gak bisa dapet closed trade real. Jadi ini gerbang.

  BARU EVOLUTION LOOP

  Setelah MT5 konek dan closed trade masuk:
  - ClosedTradeHandler ambil dari history_deals_get()
  - EvolutionScheduler trigger by trade count real
  - PerformanceScanner calculate Sharpe dari closed trade real
  - Strategy disable/enable berdasarkan PnL real, bukan simulasi

  KOMITMEN GW
